# Java Audiveris 5.11 (Temurin JDK 25) HEADS template-catalog oracle.
#
# Every page reaches the real HEADS step in a fresh JVM. Staff usage
# therefore records the actual BEAMS-derived getHeadPointSize result.
# Active shapes are the union of NoteHeadsBuilder's all/stem/hollow
# ShapeSet selectors, serialized in ShapeSet.Heads factory order.
#
# Anchors include exact Double.toHexString values, raw bits, and Java's
# asymmetric rounded Template.getOffset result. PixelDistance records
# retain factory row-major order and exact signed distance values/bits.
# Row digests are FNV-1a-64 over UTF-8 canonical records plus newline.
# Template.evaluate weights are foreground=0x1.8p2, background=0x1.0p0,
# hole=0x1.0p2; it compares only zero/nonzero after UNKNOWN filtering.
#
# Generate one target per fresh JVM, in this order: chula, allegretto,
# batuque, carmen, cucaracha, hove, zizi, BachInvention5.

headtemplateset chopin-nocturne-page23-system4-cue.png#1 kind stem count 4 shapes NOTEHEAD_VOID,NOTEHEAD_VOID_SMALL,NOTEHEAD_BLACK,NOTEHEAD_BLACK_SMALL
headtemplateset chopin-nocturne-page23-system4-cue.png#1 kind hollow count 6 shapes BREVE,BREVE_SMALL,WHOLE_NOTE,WHOLE_NOTE_SMALL,NOTEHEAD_VOID,NOTEHEAD_VOID_SMALL
